    buf1: ComputedBuffer
    buf1.layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[1632], stride=[1])
    buf1.users = [NodeUser(node=SchedulerNode(name='op2'), can_inplace=True, is_weak=False)]
]
op1.group.device = cuda:0
op1.group.iteration = (1632, 32)
op1.sizes = ([1632], [32])
primals_2_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[1632, 32], stride=[32, 1])
buf0_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[1632, 1], stride=[1, 1632])
buf1_layout = FixedLayout('cuda:0', torch.float32, size=[1632], stride=[1])
class op1_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 1632, p1: 32}
    index0 = 32*p0 + p1
    index1 = p0
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('primals_2', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf0', get_index_1)
        abs_1 = ops.abs(load_1)
        constant = ops.constant(inf, torch.float32)
        eq = ops.eq(abs_1, constant)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf0', get_index_2)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(0.0, torch.float32)
        where = ops.where(eq, constant_1, load_2)
        sub = ops.sub(load, where)
        exp = ops.exp(sub)
        reduction = ops.reduction(torch.float32, torch.float32, 'sum', exp)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index1')
        store_reduction = ops.store_reduction('buf1', get_index_3, reduction)
        return store_reduction

class op171_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 1632, p1: 32}
    index0 = 32*p0 + p1
    index1 = p0
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        load = ops.load('primals_36', get_index)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf170', get_index_1)
        abs_1 = ops.abs(load_1)
        constant = ops.constant(inf, torch.float32)
        eq = ops.eq(abs_1, constant)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index1')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf170', get_index_2)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(0.0, torch.float32)
        where = ops.where(eq, constant_1, load_2)
        sub = ops.sub(load, where)
        exp = ops.exp(sub)
        reduction = ops.reduction(torch.float32, torch.float32, 'sum', exp)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index1')
        store_reduction = ops.store_reduction('buf171', get_index_3, reduction)
        return store_reduction
